[Sudsy loon junk Shop]
The wagon is tightly packed with all manner of trinkets, knick-knacks, tankards, and useless junk. Costume jewelry and cheap tin mugs sit in wooden boxes, draped with poorly woven cloth and stiff leather. Bits of gaudy tinsel, dull quartz, and brightly painted wood, decorate the interior of the wagon. A fat, jovial Vorngar with a long beard waddles about the wagon, checking over her wares. Tin and rusty iron jewelry cover her wrists and fingers, while a string of fake pearls and diamonds glitters falsely around her neck. She smiles at you, pointing to the very "low" price of a packet of tobacco, and the light glints off of her faux-gold teeth.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: closed gaudy wagon door

[Phenton's phials and Alchemies]
A bone-beaded curtain covers the door, giving off the trickling sound of rain whenever someone passes through. A myriad of herbs, bones, hides, and other plant matter hang from the rafters of the ramshackle hut in hastily woven nets and string. A clutter of boxes, trunks, parcels, wicker baskets, and dishes stack against the walls. A cage of crickets and other insects sits atop a stack of boxes on the west wall. A wooden counter, covered in bone dust, plant matter and various colorful liquids sits in the center of the shop. A heavily wrinkled, old Silvarin sits hunched over the counter on a gnarled wooden stool. He glances up briefly, peering over his spectacles with hazy, blind eyes before continuing his work with a knife and strips of snake skin.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: closed ramshackle hut door

[Harvest Moon Road]
Black cobblestone streets are lined with hand carved lighted Jack O Lanterns. Children run through playing hide and seek, taking a moment as they try to evade their trackers, to smile at the passers by. Pretend howls and moans made by candy seeking children echo across the area.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

[Merchants Run]
The neatly manicured stone street bustles with activity as the sounds of market resonate excitedly. Travelers, merchants and city residents gather in a foray of discussion over their latest purchases, as the varying smells of cured leathers, hot metal, and baked spices mingle overhead in the air. [Merchants Run]
Smoke steadily rises from the chimneys of this massive stone building on the north side of the well maintained road. Through the open facade young Vorngar can be seen pumping the bellows while the master heats a rod of glowing iron within the forge.  The clanking sound of metal on metal can be heard as the gifted apprentices create and sharpen weapons of all types. The hot fires of the forge radiate all around. A large sign mounted above a tall studded iron door clearly reads, "Talafon's Weapons and Armor".  On the south side of the road a small colorful building reflects light cast from two glowing orbs mounted on each side of a glittering portal floating between a silver archway. Above the archway a small but functional sign reads "Malinar's Magic and Mayham".

[Southern Cross Road]
Cobblestone streets void of dirt and litter extend north and south. Sounds of chatter and laughter can be heard clearly to the north and muffled sounds of horse hooves and creaky wagon wheels to the south. The occasional city waste engineer passes by to check the large black iron trashcan siting on the eastern side of the road.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south, north


[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

Travelers, merchants and city residents gather in a foray of discussion over their latest purchases, as the varying smells of cured leathers, hot metal, and baked spices mingle overhead in the air. A small shop constructed in black shingles attempts to blend into the shadows on the southern side of the street, its large wooden door beckoning patrons to enter. A small wooden sign posted above the door reads "Double Boil."

The area is illuminated.

[Merchants Run]
Smoke steadily rises from the chimneys of this massive stone building on the north side of the well maintained road. Through the open facade young Vorngar can be seen pumping the bellows while the master heats a rod of glowing iron within the forge.  The clanking sound of metal on metal can be heard as the gifted apprentices create and sharpen weapons of all types. The hot fires of the forge radiate all around. A large sign mounted above a tall studded iron door clearly reads, "Talafon's Weapons and Armor".  On the south side of the road a small colorful building reflects light cast from two glowing orbs mounted on each side of a glittering portal floating between a silver archway. Above the archway a small but functional sign reads "Malinar's Magic and Mayham".

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: west, east, portal, open door

[Malinar's Magic and Mayhem]
The rapidly pulsing portal glows circumspectly upon etched maize-colored walls. Items display themselves suspended above ornate emerald encrusted shelves unassisted. The air, alive with sounds of wind blowing and fires burning, cuts through the room as if summoned by obscure forces. A petite female Duskwen stands behind a meticulously organized blown glass counter. With a wave of her neatly manicured hand she summons several minerals and roots and mixes them methodically in a beaker of clear fluid which begins to glow green.

The area is illuminated.

[Southern Cross Road]
Cobblestone streets void of dirt and litter extend north and south. Sounds of chatter and laughter can be heard clearly to the north and muffled sounds of horse hooves and creaky wagon wheels to the south. The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south, north


[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

In this room are: northern monument, giant wickedly grinning pumpkin, western monument, large wooden bulletin board, southeastern monument, northwestern monument, southern monument, eastern monument, southwestern monument, northeastern monument
Obvious exits are: west, southwest, south, east, north

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. [Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

[North Street]
The road slants down slightly to the north. A sign directing travelers. The sign which sits stalwart on the east side of the road reads in common "north, graveyard northwest, City of Urda". Dust and dirt is kicked up by small gusts of wind that seemingly come out of nowhere. A small vindinberry tree grows wildly on the western side of the road, its twisted and warped branches play home to a large hay birds nest.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. [Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

Your entire body begins to exude a shining light from deep within.


[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.
